# llm-d: Agent Context

Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

Related pages: [Overview](index.md), [Extracted Data](details.md), [Security](security.md), [Network](network.md), [Dataflow](dataflow.md).

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

llm-d is a Kubernetes component. deploying 6 workload(s) (llm-d-coordinator, fma-requester, render, render, interactive-pod, mooncake-master-store) with 6 container(s). exposing 8 service(s) on ports\: 50051, 50052, 6379, 8000, 8080, 9003. with 1 HorizontalPodAutoscaler(s). connecting to external services\: requests, urllib. runtime dependencies\: PostgreSQL, Redis. with 12 ingress route(s). Python k8s client with 4 API call(s). integrates with Istio, Kueue, Prometheus via labels/annotations. references components\: vllm (import). mounts 2 ConfigMap(s) as volumes\: llm-d-coordinator-config, mooncake-master-store-config.
